<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHCb实验在LHC Run 3前进行了重大升级，采用全软件触发方案，需要在30MHz碰撞频率下高效、准确地重建带电粒子径迹，以替代计算开销大的磁场查表和数值积分方法。

Method: 实现了一种快速参数化卡尔曼滤波器，用解析参数化替代昂贵的磁场图查找和数值积分，在约500块GPU上运行于第一级触发系统，保持卡尔曼滤波的数学框架不变。

Result: 相比前代部分径迹拟合算法，处理时间仅增加2%，但动量分辨率显著提升，D0和J/ψ不变质量分辨率提高约两倍；对探测器偏差更鲁棒，同时将本底拒绝能力提升一倍以上而不损失真实径迹效率。

Conclusion: 参数化卡尔曼滤波器成功替代了实时触发环境中的前代拟合算法，以极小的时间开销换取了径迹重建质量和本底抑制能力的全面显著提升。

Abstract: The LHCb experiment at the Large Hadron Collider underwent a major upgrade before the LHC Run 3 data taking period, employing an all-software approach in its trigger system. Here we present a fast implementation of a Kalman filter, used in the first trigger stage since the 2025 data taking period, allowing to determine parameter estimates of charged-particle trajectories at the full LHCb collision frequency of 30 MHz. This approach replaces computationally expensive magnetic field map lookups and numerical integration methods with fast analytical parameterisations while maintaining the mathematical framework of Kalman filtering. Implemented on approximately 500 GPUs within the first-level trigger, the algorithm has replaced the previous partial track fitting algorithm in the real-time trigger environment at the cost of a 2% increase in processing time. Compared to the previous fitter this parameterised Kalman filter shows a significantly improved momentum resolution, resulting in a factor of two improvement in the invariant mass resolutions for reconstructed D0 and J/ψ hadrons. It additionally demonstrates greater robustness against detector misalignment effects and substantially sharpens the discrimination between genuine particle trajectories and accidental background, more than doubling the rejection of the latter at no cost to genuine-track efficiency, for a standard selection working point.

</details>


### [99] [A Differential Neutral-to-Charged Current Double-Proton Observable for Studying Short-Range Correlations in Neutrino--Argon Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14942)
*A. Bueno,D. Garcia-Gamez,C. Martin-Morales*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出利用中微子-氩相互作用中中性流与带电流两质子产生比率作为质子对相对动量的函数，通过SRC对带电流的增强效应来探测短程关联成分，该比率对通量归一化和探测器系统误差不敏感，可在现有液氩时间投影室中实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两核子发射是中微子-原子核相互作用建模中的主要不确定性来源，但目前尚无中微子测量能够约束其短程关联（SRC）成分，因此需要提出一种可行的实验观测量来直接探测SRC贡献。

Method: 利用液氩时间投影室，测量中微子-氩反应中中性流（NC）与带电流（CC）两质子产生事件的比率作为质子对相对动量的函数。CC相互作用将丰沛的中子-质子SRC对转换为可见的质子对，而NC中质子-质子SRC对约少20倍，因此比率在高相对动量处下降，下降幅度与SRC份额成比例。

Result: 模拟预测该比率在高相对动量区域出现抑制，抑制幅度随SRC份额标度变化。通量归一化和关联探测器系统误差在比率中大幅抵消，使得该测量在现有实验条件下可行。

Conclusion: 该观测量独立于精确的SRC贡献，首次提供了弱两核子发射同位旋结构的直接实验探针。

Abstract: Two-nucleon emission is a leading uncertainty in neutrino--nucleus interaction modeling, and no neutrino measurement constrains its short-range-correlation (SRC) component. We propose such a measurement: the ratio of neutral-current to charged-current two-proton production in neutrino--argon interactions as a function of the proton-pair relative momentum. Charged-current interactions convert the abundant neutron--proton SRC pairs into visible proton pairs, whereas neutral-current interactions lack an analogous SRC contribution because proton--proton pairs are about twenty times less abundant. The ratio is therefore predicted to decrease at large relative momentum, where the charged-current SRC contribution dominates, while the neutral-current sample provides a smooth reference determined mainly by final-state interactions and multinucleon processes. Simulations predict a suppression whose magnitude scales with the SRC fraction. Flux normalization and correlated detector systematics largely cancel in the ratio, making the measurement feasible with existing liquid-argon time projection chambers. Independently of the precise SRC contribution, this observable provides the first direct experimental probe of the isospin structure of weak two-nucleon emission.

</details>


### [100] [The study of bulk damage of high gamma-irradiated $n^+$-in-$p$ silicon diodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15056)
*M. Mikestikova,P. Federicova,P. Gallus,R. Jirasek,J. Kozakova,J. Kroll,J. Kvasnicka,V. Latonova,I. Mandic,K. Masek,P. Novotny,R. Privara,P. Tuma,I. Zatocilova*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了高总电离剂量（0.50-8.28 MGy）伽马辐照对p型硅传感器的体损伤效应，发现漏电流随剂量线性增加（损伤系数依赖于电阻率和氧浓度），有效掺杂浓度和全耗尽电压先降后升，初始下降归因于受主去除效应，且未观察到退火效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表征伽马辐照引起的位移损伤，探究不同氧浓度和电阻率下p型硅传感器在高总电离剂量下的损伤演化规律。

Method: 制备不同氧浓度和电阻率的n⁺-in-p硅二极管，采用SIMS测定氧浓度，60Co伽马源辐照至0.50-8.28 MGy总剂量，80分钟60°C退火，通过IV/CV特性、全耗尽电压演化及瞬态电流技术（TCT）提取电场分布和空间电荷符号。

Result: 漏电流随总剂量线性增加，损伤系数与初始电阻率和氧浓度相关；有效掺杂浓度和全耗尽电压先显著下降后回升；未观察到退火效应。

Conclusion: 伽马辐照在p型硅中引起显著的体损伤，初始有效掺杂浓度下降由受主去除效应主导，漏电流与CV特性对退火不敏感。

Abstract: The bulk damage of $p$-type silicon sensors caused by gamma irradiation with high total ionizing doses has been investigated. The study was carried out on different types of $n^+$-in-$p$ silicon diodes with different oxygen concentrations and silicon bulk resistivities. The Secondary-Ion Mass Spectrometry technique was used to determine the relative concentration of oxygen in the individual samples. The measured diodes were irradiated by a $^{60}$Co gamma source to total ionizing doses ranging from 0.50 up to 8.28 MGy, and annealed for 80 minutes at 60°C. The main goal of the study was to characterize the gamma-radiation induced displacement damage by measuring IV and CV characteristics, and the evolution of the full depletion voltage with the total ionizing dose. The Transient Current Technique was used to verify the full depletion voltage and to extract the electric field distribution and the sign of the space charge in the silicon diodes irradiated to the lowest and the highest delivered total ionizing doses.
  The results show a linear increase of the bulk leakage current with the total ionizing dose, with the damage coefficient being dependent on initial resistivity and oxygen concentration of the silicon diode. The effective doping concentration and full depletion voltage decrease significantly with an increasing total ionizing dose, before starting to increase again at a specific dose. We assume that the initial decrease in the effective doping concentration is caused by the effect of acceptor removal. An additional notable finding of this study is that the bulk leakage current and CV characteristics of the gamma-irradiated diodes do not show any evidence of an annealing effect.

</details>

### [101] [Tensor Network Methods for Advection-Diffusion-Reaction Systems Using Quantum-Inspired Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14150)
*Nahid Binandeh Dehaghani,Rafal Wisniewski,A. Pedro Aguiar*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于量子启发张量网络的框架求解对流-扩散-反应偏微分方程，将离散化解场编码为矩阵乘积态，微分算子编码为矩阵乘积算子，时间积分在张量网络形式下通过显式欧拉更新和可控截断完成。在一维和二维问题上的数值实验表明，该方法在保持紧凑、稳定和精度的同时，特征维数保持较小，展示了张量网络作为高效结构保持工具求解多维PDE的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的PDE数值方法在高维问题中面临维度灾难，计算和存储成本随维度指数增长。张量网络作为一种量子启发的方法，有望通过低秩结构高效表示高维数据，但目前将其系统性地应用于对流-扩散-反应方程的时间积分尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 用矩阵乘积态编码离散化解场，用矩阵乘积算子表示微分算子，在张量网络形式下执行显式欧拉时间积分，并在每一步进行可控截断以维持张量网络的低秩结构。

Result: 在一维和二维对流-扩散-反应问题中，与高精度龙格-库塔参考解相比，该方法保持了紧凑性、稳定性和准确性，特征维数在整个模拟过程中保持较小。

Conclusion: 张量网络作为结构保持工具在多维PDE模拟中具有巨大潜力，能有效解决维度灾难问题。

Abstract: We present a quantum-inspired tensor-network framework for solving advection-diffusion-reaction (ADR) partial differential equations. Discretized solution fields are encoded as matrix product states (MPS), while differential operators are represented as matrix product operators (MPOs). Time integration is performed entirely in tensor-network form using explicit Euler updates with controlled truncation. The method is evaluated on one- and two-dimensional ADR problems and compared with high-accuracy Runge-Kutta reference solutions. Numerical results show that the proposed representation remains compact, stable, and accurate across a range of dynamical regimes. The solver captures both local solution profiles and global observables while maintaining small bond dimensions throughout the simulation. These results highlight the potential of tensor networks as efficient structure-preserving tools for PDE simulation in multiple spatial dimensions.

</details>


### [102] [NGMRES convergence analysis and proof of acceleration for contractive and noncontractive iterations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14268)
*Y. He,L. Rebholz,M. Xiao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文首次对非线性GMRES（NGMRES）应用于收缩和非收缩不动点迭代的收敛性进行了分析，证明了优化问题的比率增益是加速（或实现）收敛的机制，并揭示了预测线性收敛速度的关键量，数值实验验证了理论结果并展示了NGMRES相比Anderson加速的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前缺乏对NGMRES应用于一般非线性系统时收敛性的严格理论分析，尤其是在非收缩不动点迭代场景下的加速机制尚不明确。

Method: 对收缩和非收缩两种情形下NGMRES的收敛性进行理论分析，识别优化问题中的比率增益作为加速收敛的核心机制，并推导出预测每次迭代线性收敛速度的关键量。

Result: 证明了比率增益是加速/实现收敛的机制；发现了一个直接预测线性收敛速度的量，且该量至多为1，仅高阶项导致非收敛；数值实验验证了理论准确性，展示了加速效果、自适应深度选择、重启策略对非收缩迭代的改进、多解PDE问题上的适用性，以及超线性FPI中优于Anderson加速的表现。

Conclusion: NGMRES在收缩和非收缩不动点迭代中均有理论保证的加速收敛能力，所揭示的预测量为自适应算法设计提供了新工具，且在实际复杂问题中优于Anderson加速方法。

Abstract: This paper gives the first convergence analysis and proof of acceleration for nonlinear GMRES (NGMRES) applied to contractive and noncontractive fixed point iterations (FPIs) for solving general nonlinear systems. Our main results are that in both the contractive and noncontractive cases, the ratio gain of the optimization problem is the mechanism responsible for accelerating (or enabling) convergence. Our analysis also reveals a second important quantity related to the optimization problem, which directly predicts the linear convergence rate at each iteration and proves it is at most 1; hence only higher order terms are responsible for NGMRES non-convergence. Numerical results for several challenging nonlinear test problems are given that illustrate the theory, show how the acceleration improves convergence, show that the quantity predicting the linear convergence rate is remarkably accurate and moreover can be useful for adaptively choosing NGMRES depth, show how restarts can improve convergence in noncontractive iterations, show how NGMRES is naturally suited for finding distinct solutions of a multi-solution PDE, and that NGMRES can perform better than Anderson acceleration when applied to superlinear FPIs.

</details>


### [103] [A structure-preserving Numerical Method for the Compressible Resistive-Hall-MHD System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14286)
*Murtazo Nazarov,Rafael Rodriguez-Velasco,Ignacio Tomas*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于可压缩电阻性Hall-磁流体动力学(Hall-MHD)模型的保结构方法，将微分算子拆分为流体部分和磁场部分，分别使用连续Lagrange元与旋度协调有限元空间离散，实现了密度和内能正性保持、总能量守恒、比熵最小原理以及散度约束保持，并通过多个基准算例验证了方法的鲁棒性和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对可压缩电阻性Hall-MHD模型，现有数值方法在保持物理约束（如正性、能量守恒、熵原理、散度约束）方面存在不足，需要一种兼顾结构保持和计算鲁棒性的数值格式。

Method: 将微分算子分裂为Euler流体部分和磁场部分；流体部分用连续Lagrange有限元离散，采用SSP-RK显式时间推进；磁场部分用旋度协调(Nédélec)有限元离散，采用Crank-Nicholson隐式格式配合Newton迭代求解；引入高阶人工电阻率改善非线性残差和Jacobian矩阵的适定性。

Result: 成功求解了光滑哨声波、Orszag-Tang涡（对比电阻MHD与Hall-MHD）以及磁重联问题，验证了方法在鲁棒性和精度方面的有效性；给出了Newton迭代Jacobian矩阵的强制性估计。

Conclusion: 所提出的分裂格式能够同时保证流体部分的正性、能量守恒、熵原理以及磁场部分的散度无源约束，为Hall-MHD模型的保结构数值求解提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a structure-preserving method for the compressible resistive Hall-magnetohydrodynamics (MHD) model. The differential operator is split into two parts: a hydrodynamic part consisting of the compressible Euler equations, and a magnetic part consisting of a system coupling the Lorentz force and the induction equation. The method uses continuous Lagrange elements for the Euler part and a curl-conforming finite element space for the magnetic part. The hydrodynamic part preserves the positivity of the density and internal energy, the conservation of total energy, and the minimum principle for the specific entropy. Owing to the choice of finite elements, the magnetic part preserves the divergence involution constraint. The fluid part is solved using explicit strong-stability-preserving Runge-Kutta (SSP-RK) methods, whereas the magnetic part is solved by Crank-Nicholson method, which requires using Newton's method. Coercivity estimates for the Jacobian of the corresponding Newton iteration are presented. We introduce a high-order artificial resistivity to improve the conditioning of the nonlinear residual and the invertibility of the Jacobian. Several challenging benchmarks, including a smooth whistler wave, the Orszag-Tang vortex for comparing resistive MHD with resistive Hall-MHD, and a magnetic reconnection problem, are solved to validate the robustness and accuracy of the method.

</details>


### [104] [Neural Very Weak Formulations enabling Hardware-Oriented deep PDE solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14498)
*Gabriel Acosta,Francisco Bersetche*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最小二乘非常弱形式的神经网络方法用于求解椭圆问题，通过使用光滑测试函数和低正则性试验函数（如阶梯激活函数和单比特量化线性函数），避免了自动微分，在奇异性解和高维问题中表现良好，且适合硬件高效实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于神经网络的偏微分方程求解方法通常需要自动微分，计算成本高。作者希望探索一种避免自动微分、同时适用于低正则性神经网络的离散化框架，并验证其在奇异性解和高维问题中的有效性。

Method: 采用最小二乘非常弱形式的变分框架，试验函数使用低正则性神经网络（如基于阶梯激活和单比特量化线性函数的网络），测试函数则选自适当光滑的空间，从而避免对试验函数求导。

Result: 该方法在多种神经网络空间上均表现良好，尤其在奇异性解和高维场景下仍能保持较好的性能；基于阶梯激活和单比特量化线性函数的试验函数特别适合硬件高效实现。

Conclusion: 证明最小二乘非常弱形式的神经网络方法可以有效求解椭圆问题，避免自动微分，并支持低正则性试验函数，在挑战性问题上具有鲁棒性，且具备硬件友好性。

Abstract: We show, as a proof of concept, that least-squares very weak formulations of elliptic problems can be effectively discretized by neural networks possessing low regularity, provided the test functions are drawn from appropriately smooth spaces. Apart from the immediate computational benefit of avoiding automatic differentiation, this approach, evaluated across various neural network spaces, demonstrates good performance even in challenging contexts, such as singular solutions and high dimensional settings. Particular attention is paid to trial functions based on step activations and one bit quantized linear functions, which are amenable to efficient hardware-oriented implementations.

</details>


### [105] [Steering dynamic network centrality via control theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14610)
*Fabio Durastante,Beatrice Meini,Luca Saluzzi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对时间演化网络（时序网络）的动态中心性控制框架，通过庞特里亚金最大值原理求解最优控制问题，以最小化网络结构修改实现节点中心性向目标状态的引导，并采用Krylov方法进行大规模问题的近似计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序网络在生物学、社会科学和信息技术等领域广泛存在，其拓扑和边权重随时间动态变化，影响信息流、交通效率和神经活动等过程。理解并控制这些网络对于预测未来行为和优化动态过程至关重要，而现有的中心性控制研究多局限于静态网络，缺乏对时序网络动态特性的处理。

Method: 将动态中心性控制问题形式化为一个基于常微分方程（矩阵形式或向量形式）的最优控制问题，控制作用施加在网络边上；利用庞特里亚金最大值原理导出最优性条件并进行高效求解；针对大规模问题，采用Krylov子空间技术近似计算矩阵-函数乘积，避免显式构造稠密矩阵函数。

Result: 在合成数据和真实时序网络上的数值实验表明，所提出的框架能够在预设的控制约束下有效地将接收中心性（receive centrality）引导至期望状态。

Conclusion: 该工作将静态网络中的中心性控制问题推广到时序网络场景，提供了理论框架和高效数值方法，为实际动态网络的控制与优化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Time-evolving networks, or temporal networks, play a crucial role in modeling dynamic interactions across various domains, including biology, social sciences, and information technology. Unlike static networks, these systems undergo continuous changes in topology and edge weights, influencing processes such as information flow, transportation efficiency, and neural activity. Understanding and controlling these networks are essential for predicting future behavior and optimizing dynamic processes. This work focuses on the problem of dynamic centrality, a measure of node importance in time-dependent networks. Specifically, we address how to steer network centrality to a desired state by making minimal modifications to the network structure. This problem is formulated as an optimal control problem for an ordinary differential equation, either matrix- or vector-based, where the control acts on network edges. The proposed framework generalizes centrality control problems studied in static networks and leverages the Pontryagin Maximum Principle for efficient solutions. For large-scale problems, the required matrix-function actions are approximated by Krylov-type techniques, avoiding the explicit formation of dense matrix functions. Numerical experiments on synthetic and real temporal networks show that the proposed framework can effectively steer receive centrality under prescribed control constraints.

</details>


### [106] [Gradient-enhanced spline dimensional decomposition for uncertainty quantification with limited training samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14615)
*Eunho Heo,Dongjin Lee*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出梯度增强样条维度分解（GE-SDD），通过融合函数值与偏导数信息，结合Frobenius范数平衡、岭回归与分组K折交叉验证，有效解决高维工程响应中训练数据有限导致的病态系数估计问题，在多个基准测试中优于标准SDD和梯度增强Kriging。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准样条维度分解（SDD）在处理高维工程响应中的局部特征和复杂非线性时表现良好，但有限训练数据会导致函数值系数估计严重病态，亟需利用梯度信息改善条件数。

Method: 1) 利用函数值和偏导数联合训练代理模型；2) 构造对角行权重矩阵，按Frobenius范数平衡函数与导数块；3) 在概率加权Sobolev坐标下通过岭回归求解平衡系统；4) 采用分组K折交叉验证选择正则化参数以防止信息泄漏；5) 将解映射回L2正交SDD基以保持均值和方差闭式估计。

Result: 在二维连续指数函数、三参数线性动力系统和30维25杆桁架问题上：GE-SDD优于标准SDD；对非光滑基准，中位数NRMSE为1.022%（Kriging为8.731%）；桁架问题中训练量适中以上时，GE-SDD的NRMSE和标准差估计均优于Kriging。

Conclusion: 梯度增强的效果取决于输入维度、基分辨率、训练规模及目标UQ量；GE-SDD是梯度利用更稳健的代理建模方法。

Abstract: A spline dimensional decomposition (SDD) surrogate effectively represents high-dimensional engineering responses with localized features and complex nonlinearities in uncertainty quantification (UQ). However, limited training data can make coefficient estimation from function values severely ill-conditioned. We propose gradient-enhanced SDD (GE-SDD), which trains the surrogate using function values and partial derivatives. A diagonal row-weight matrix balances the function and derivative blocks by their Frobenius norms. We solve the balanced system through ridge regression in probability-weighted Sobolev coordinates and select the regularization parameter using grouped K-fold cross-validation to prevent information leakage. Mapping the solution back to the L2-orthonormal SDD basis preserves closed-form mean and variance estimates. We evaluate the proposed GE-SDD on a two-dimensional continuous exponential function, a linear dynamical system with three uncertain parameters, and a 30-dimensional 25-bar truss. GE-SDD is more accurate than standard SDD and uses gradients more robustly than gradient-enhanced Kriging. GE-SDD achieves a median NRMSE of 1.022% on the nonsmooth benchmark, compared with 8.731% for Kriging. For the truss, GE-SDD yields lower NRMSE and more accurate standard-deviation estimates than Kriging at moderate training sizes and above. Overall, the benefits of gradient augmentation depend on input dimension, basis resolution, training size, and the target UQ quantity.

</details>


### [107] [A new strategy for physics-informed neural networks based on hierarchical collocation point refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14665)
*Minjae Choi,Dukhwan Shin,Youngsoo Yang,Eunjung Lee*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: MPU-PINNs提出了一种从粗到细的多网格训练策略，通过逐步增加配点数量并传递学习参数，在保持精度的同时显著减少PINNs的训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PINNs在需要大量配点来精确强制执行控制方程时，训练计算成本过高。

Method: 采用多网格粗到细的训练策略：先在粗糙配点集上训练网络，再将学习到的参数传递到更精细的层级；同时引入缩放技术缓解高频问题中的谱偏差。

Result: 在二维/三维泊松方程、对流扩散反应方程和亥姆霍兹方程等基准PDE上，MPU-PINNs大幅减少了训练时间，同时达到与传统PINNs、SA-PINNs和XPINNs相当的精度。

Conclusion: MPU-PINNs提供了一个高效的单网络训练框架，增强了PINNs的计算效率和可扩展性，从粗到细的学习策略显著减少了精细层级所需的优化工作量。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) offer a flexible framework for solving partial differential equations (PDEs), but training can become computationally expensive when a large number of collocation points are required to accurately enforce the governing equations. To alleviate this cost, we introduce multigrid-based parameter-updated PINNs (MPU-PINNs), a coarse-to-fine training strategy that progressively increases the number of training points throughout the learning process. The proposed approach begins by training a neural network on a coarse set of collocation points and then transfers the learned parameters to successively finer levels. This initialization strategy enables the network to capture the global features of the solution at a relatively low computational cost before refining local details with additional training points. To further improve performance for high-frequency problems, we incorporate a scaling technique that mitigates the effects of spectral bias during training. We evaluate MPU-PINNs on several benchmark PDEs, including two- and three-dimensional Poisson equations, a convection-diffusion-reaction equation, and the Helmholtz equation. Numerical experiments indicate that MPU-PINNs greatly reduce training time while achieving accuracy comparable to that of conventional PINNs and other representative variants such as SA-PINNs and XPINNs. The results further suggest that the proposed coarse-to-fine learning strategy substantially decreases the optimization effort required at finer levels. Overall, MPU-PINNs provide an efficient single-network training framework that enhances the computational efficiency and scalability of PINNs for a broad range of PDE problems.

</details>


### [108] [Operator-Split Bayesian Learning for Elliptic PDEs with Unequal Interior and Boundary Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14680)
*Emmanuel E. Oguadimma*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种算子分裂贝叶斯学习框架，用于解决内点和边界观测数量不相等情况下的二阶一致椭圆Dirichlet问题。通过为源项和边界值分配独立贝叶斯神经网络先验，将乘积后验推过椭圆解算子，证明了该后验向真实解收缩，并给出了近极小极大上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决内点和边界观测数量不相等时，二阶椭圆Dirichlet问题的不确定性量化与贝叶斯推断问题，填补现有理论在非平衡采样预算下的空白。

Method: 采用算子分裂方法，分别为源项和边界值赋予独立的贝叶斯神经网络先验，构造乘积后验，再通过椭圆解算子推演得到解的后验分布。理论分析证明后验收缩性，数值实验验证不确定性传播。

Result: 后验分布以分离的收缩半径向真实解收缩，域贡献由二阶椭圆算子控制，边界贡献由边界内在维度控制；导出近极小极大上界（至多相差对数因子）。

Conclusion: 该框架有效处理了内点和边界观测数量不等时的贝叶斯反问题，理论上达到了近最优的收敛速率，数值实验验证了源和边界不确定性的传播以及采样预算差异的影响。

Abstract: We propose an operator-split Bayesian learning framework for second-order uniformly elliptic Dirichlet problems with unequal numbers of interior and boundary observations. The data consist of noisy measurements of the source in the domain and noisy measurements of the boundary values. Independent Bayesian neural-network (BNN) priors are assigned to these two quantities, and the resulting product posterior is pushed forward through the elliptic solution operator. We prove that the posterior induced by this construction contracts around the true solution. The contraction radius separates a domain contribution, governed by the second-order elliptic operator, from a boundary contribution, governed by the intrinsic dimension of the boundary. Together with the minimax lower bound of \cite{ZhaoLu2026}, this yields a near-minimax upper bound up to logarithmic factors. Our numerical experiments illustrate the propagation of source and boundary uncertainty and the effects of unequal sampling budgets on the posterior reconstruction.

</details>


### [109] [A low-rank hierarchical framework for the non-Markovian stochastic Schrödinger equation with convergence analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14689)
*Zhuohan Zhang,Zhenning Cai*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种基于低秩近似浴关联函数的非马尔可夫随机薛定谔方程（NMSSE）数值框架，通过将记忆核分解为有限维表示，推导出截断的分层方程组，在计算可行性和物理保真度之间取得平衡。在温和假设下建立了分层框架的严格收敛性分析，并证明该框架是纯态层次方程（HOPS）的数学推广。数值实验验证了方法的有效性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非马尔可夫随机薛定谔方程（NMSSE）的数值求解面临计算复杂度和物理保真度之间的权衡难题，现有方法（如HOPS）在表示非马尔可夫效应方面存在灵活性不足的问题。本文旨在提出一个更具通用性的低秩近似框架，既能降低计算负担，又能保持对记忆效应的准确描述。

Method: 采用低秩近似对浴关联函数（记忆核）进行分解，将其表示为有限维表示，从而推导出截断的分层方程组。该框架在数学上推广了HOPS方法，通过控制截断误差实现计算效率与物理精度的平衡，并基于温和假设给出了收敛性分析。

Result: 建立了分层框架在温和假设下的严格收敛性分析；证明该框架是HOPS的数学推广，具有更灵活的非马尔可夫效应表示能力；通过多个基准模型的数值实验验证了方法的有效性和计算精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的低秩近似框架为NMSSE的数值求解提供了一种高效且严格收敛的新方案，在保持物理保真度的同时显著提升了计算可行性，并为非马尔可夫量子动力学模拟提供了更灵活的理论工具。

Abstract: We propose and analyze a novel numerical framework for the non-Markovian stochastic Schrödinger equation (NMSSE) based on a low-rank approximation of the bath correlation functions. By decomposing the memory kernel into a finite-dimensional representation, we derive a truncated system of hierarchical equations that effectively balances computational tractability with physical fidelity. A rigorous convergence analysis is established for the hierarchical framework under mild assumptions. We demonstrate that our formulation serves as a mathematical generalization of the Hierarchy of Pure States (HOPS), encompassing it as a special case while offering a more flexible representation of non-Markovian effects. Numerical experiments across several benchmark models are presented to illustrate the validity and efficacy of the proposed method.

</details>


### [110] [Newton-Based Mixed Precision Iterative Refinement for Large-Scale Sparse Continuous-Time Algebraic Riccati Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14742)
*Juan Zhang,Yang Zhou*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于牛顿法的混合精度迭代细化框架，用于求解大规模稀疏连续时间代数Riccati方程（CARE），通过低精度求解初始近似和内部Lyapunov修正方程、高精度计算残差和更新解，在保证精度的同时提升计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模稀疏CARE问题的求解计算开销大，传统全双精度方法效率低，需要在不损失精度的情况下加速计算。

Method: 提出牛顿法混合精度迭代细化框架：低精度计算初始近似和内部Lyapunov修正方程，高精度计算残差和更新解；引入因子分解与截断策略处理不定残差和牛顿修正项，保持半正定性并控制秩增长；基于一阶舍入误差分析建立残差递推关系，推导出混合精度细化的Lyapunov算子条件阈值；给出基于ADI（NLR-ADI和LR-ADI）的具体实现。

Result: 数值实验表明：在稠密CARE上验证了误差分析给出的条件效应；在大规模稀疏CARE上，混合精度框架比全双精度实现更快，同时保持相同精度水平。

Conclusion: 混合精度迭代细化框架能有效加速大规模稀疏CARE求解，理论分析与实验结果一致，为实际工程应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We propose a Newton-based mixed precision iterative refinement framework for solving large-scale sparse continuous-time algebraic Riccati equations (CAREs). The framework computes the initial approximation and the inner Lyapunov correction equations in lower precision, while evaluating residuals and updating the solution in higher precision. To handle indefinite residuals and Newton correction terms in low-rank form, we introduce factor decomposition procedures with truncation strategies that preserve positive semidefiniteness and control rank growth. A first-order rounding error analysis derives a residual recurrence for the refinement process and relates stable mixed precision refinement to a Lyapunov operator conditioning threshold governed by the unit roundoff of the lower precision inner solves. We then present a concrete ADI-based realization, using NLR-ADI for the initial CARE approximation and LR-ADI for the inner Lyapunov correction equations. Compared with dense Lyapunov correction implementations, this realization reduces the main computations to shifted linear solves and low-rank factor operations, and we provide a solver-dependent complexity analysis. Numerical experiments on dense CARE over a range of condition numbers illustrate the conditioning effect described by the error analysis, and experiments on large-scale sparse CAREs show that the mixed precision framework is faster than the full double precision implementation while maintaining the same level of accuracy.

</details>


### [111] [On Hyperbolic Stochastic Galerkin Projections of Shallow Water Linearised Moment Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14750)
*Safiere Kuijpers,Julian Koellermeier*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种侵入式随机Galerkin公式化方法，用于一维浅水线性化矩方程，采用广义多项式混沌展开的伪谱乘积。针对不确定摩擦系数，推导了能量方程，分析了双曲性并引入正则化保证线性情况下的双曲性。数值测试表明，与蒙特卡洛方法相比，该方法在保持相当精度的同时显著提高了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准浅水方程精度有限，而浅水线性化矩方程通过增加方程数量可提升精度；同时需处理摩擦系数这一不确定参数，因此需要一种高效的不确定性量化方法。

Method: 采用侵入式随机Galerkin方法，将广义多项式混沌展开与伪谱乘积结合，对一维浅水线性化矩方程进行随机Galerkin公式化；推导能量方程，分析并正则化双曲性。

Result: 数值测试表明，所提出的随机Galerkin公式化在与非侵入式蒙特卡洛方法相比时，能够达到相当的精度，但运行时间显著更快。

Conclusion: 随机Galerkin方法在保持精度的前提下，可大幅提升含参数不确定性的浅水线性化矩方程求解效率，是一种有效的替代方案。

Abstract: In this work, we present an intrusive stochastic Galerkin formulation of the one-dimensional shallow water linearised moment equations expressed in conservative variables, using the pseudospectral product for generalised polynomial chaos expansions. The shallow water linearised moment equations constitute a hyperbolic system of partial differential equations with an arbitrary number of equations that enhance the accuracy of the standard shallow water equations. Without loss of generality, we assume for both the theoretical analysis and the simulations that the uncertain parameter is the friction coefficient. For the new stochastic Galerkin shallow water linearised moment equations, we derive an energy equation, analyse the hyperbolicity - since this property is not preserved by the stochastic Galerkin projection - and introduce a regularisation to ensure hyperbolicity for the linear case of the shallow water linearised moment equations. Through numerical tests, we demonstrate the accuracy of the new stochastic Galerkin formulation in comparison with a non-intrusive Monte Carlo method, showing that the stochastic Galerkin approach achieves comparable accuracy with significantly faster run times.

</details>


### [112] [Structure-preserving Lagrange-multiplier methods for mean curvature flow and their error bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14787)
*Balázs Kovács,Buyang Li,Christian Lubich*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一类针对闭曲面平均曲率流的演化曲面有限元方法，通过引入拉格朗日乘子保持能量递减结构，并证明了最优阶误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平均曲率流数值方法难以同时保证能量递减结构与高效计算，本文旨在通过拉格朗日乘子技术实现这一目标。

Method: 基于Huisken演化方程，采用线性隐式BDF格式离散抛物型几何变量，隐式Adams格式更新节点位置，引入拉格朗日乘子确保每一步面积递减，并支持最小变形率型人工切向速度。

Result: 证明了离散拉格朗日乘子的局部存在唯一性及简化牛顿迭代的收敛性；在强正则性假设下导出$H^1$范数中最优阶误差界$h^k+τ^q$（$k\ge 2$, $2\le q\le 5$）；数值实验验证了收敛率，且拉格朗日乘子修正的计算开销极小且与网格和时间步长无关。

Conclusion: 所提方法能严格保持能量递减结构，具备最优收敛阶，计算成本低，适用于平均曲率流的高保真数值模拟。

Abstract: We propose and analyze a class of evolving surface finite element methods for mean curvature flow of closed surfaces using Lagrange multipliers for preserving the energy-decreasing structure. The algorithm is based on the solution-driven formulation using Huisken's evolution equations for the normal vector and the mean curvature. The time discretizations use linearly implicit backward difference formulas (BDF) for the parabolic geometric variables and implicit Adams updates for the nodal positions. The approach also accommodates artificial tangential velocities of minimal-deformation-rate type. The resulting fully discrete algorithms are area-decreasing at every time step, with a prescribed decay rate determined by the computed mean curvature. We prove local existence and uniqueness of the discrete Lagrange multiplier and convergence of a simplified Newton iteration for its computation under weak regularity assumptions. Under stronger regularity assumptions, as used in the convergence theory for the underlying evolving surface finite element method, we derive optimal-order error bounds of order $h^k+τ^q$ in the $H^1$-norm for finite elements of polynomial degree $k\ge 2$ and $q$-step BDF and $q$-step implicit Adams methods with $2\le q\le 5$, both without and with minimal-deformation-rate tangential motion. Numerical experiments for mean curvature flow of a sphere confirm the predicted convergence rates and show that the Lagrange-multiplier correction entails only a small computational overhead that is essentially independent of the mesh size and the time step size.

</details>


### [113] [An unfitted boundary algebraic equation method with static-dynamic reduction for evolving implicit geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14808)
*Yizhen Huang,Qing Xia*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种静态-动态边界约化方法，用于无拟合格子格林函数法在预设移动平面域上的重复椭圆求解。该方法采用"先离散再表示"的策略，避免边界网格和奇异求积，将静止几何与运动引起的相互作用分离，并在模拟中复用不变部分，仅更新涉及变化边界的耦合。主要贡献是实现了针对平移、变形、出现和拓扑变化障碍物的更新策略并进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在移动界面模拟、设计和反应性导航中，反复求解椭圆方程时，即使使用固定笛卡尔网格避免重新划分网格，每次配置都需要重建所有边界交互，严重限制了求解效率。现有方法需要频繁更新边界交互，计算开销大。

Method: 提出静态-动态边界约化方法：1) 采用"先离散再表示"（discretize-then-represent）策略，先离散笛卡尔算子再形成格林表示，避免边界网格和奇异求积；2) 分离静止几何相关交互与运动影响的交互；3) 复用模拟中不变部分，仅更新涉及变化边界的耦合；4) 在真实界面交点处施加边界条件，复用晶格核数据，通过快速正弦变换求解器重构内部场。

Result: 实现并验证了针对平移、变形、出现和拓扑变化障碍物的更新策略，证明了该方法在处理各类动态边界问题上的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过分离静态和动态边界交互，显著提高了移动边界域上重复椭圆求解的效率，避免了传统方法中每次配置都需要完全重建边界交互的问题，为移动界面模拟提供了高效的计算框架。

Abstract: Repeated elliptic solves on domains with evolving boundaries arise in moving-interface simulation, design, and reactive navigation. Even when a fixed Cartesian grid avoids remeshing, rebuilding all boundary interactions for every configuration can limit the efficiency of repeated solves. We develop a static--dynamic boundary reduction for an unfitted lattice Green's function method on prescribed moving planar domains. Like boundary integral and boundary element methods, the formulation reduces the problem to boundary-supported unknowns through a Green representation. Its construction, however, reverses the usual order: the Cartesian operator is discretized before the Green representation is formed, rather than representing the continuous problem first and then discretizing the boundary. This discretize-then-represent viewpoint avoids boundary meshes and singular quadrature. The method also separates interactions associated with stationary geometry from those affected by motion, reuses the invariant part throughout a simulation, and updates only couplings involving the changing boundary. Boundary conditions are imposed at true interface intersections, lattice-kernel data are reused, and the interior field is reconstructed by a fast sine-transform solver. The principal contribution is an implemented and validated update strategy for translating, deforming, appearing, and topology-changing obstacles.

</details>


### [114] [Strong error analysis of a temporal approximation for stochastic Korteweg-de Vries equation with small additive noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14813)
*Jianbo Cui,Raffaele D'Ambrosio,Stefano Di Giovacchino,Liying Sun*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究小噪声条件下随机Korteweg-de Vries方程的时间强近似，通过将解分解为确定性KdV流和随机分量，再对线性化方程进行傅里叶分析，首次给出了显式强收敛率：在H¹正则性下为O(max(ε², τ, ετ^{1/2}))，在H²正则性下为O(max(ε², τ))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机KdV方程的时间逼近强误差分析因非线性项中的附加导数项以及缺乏精确解的指数矩界而极具挑战性，现有方法难以处理。

Method: 利用小噪声正则性，首先将解分解为确定性KdV流和随机分量；然后对所得随机方程进行线性化，并利用傅里叶分析技术近似线性化方程；最后组合小噪声线性化误差、线性化方程的离散化误差和确定性时间近似误差。

Result: 在H¹正则性下获得了O(max(ε², τ, ετ^{1/2}))的强收敛率，在H²正则性下获得了O(max(ε², τ))的强收敛率。

Conclusion: 首次为随机KdV方程的数值时间近似建立了显式强收敛率。

Abstract: We study strong temporal approximation of periodic stochastic Korteweg--de Vries equation driven by small additive \(Q\)-Wiener noise of amplitude \(\mathcal O(\varepsilon)\), \(0<\varepsilon\ll1\). Strong error analysis for temporal approximations of stochastic KdV is a challenging problem, due to the additional derivative term in the nonlinearity and thanks to the lack of suitable exponential moment bounds for the exact solutions. Exploiting the small-noise regime, we first decompose the solution into a deterministic KdV flow and a stochastic component; then we linearize the obtained stochastic equation and approximate the resulting equation by means of Fourier analytic techniques. Combining the small-noise linearization error, the discretization error of the linearized equation, and the deterministic temporal approximation error, we prove strong convergence rates of order \(\mathcal O(\max(\varepsilon^2,τ,\varepsilonτ^{1/2}))\) under \(H^1\)-regularity and \(\mathcal O(\max(\varepsilon^2,τ))\) under \(H^2\)-regularity, for the obtained approximation of the original stochastic KdV. To the best of our knowledge, these are the first explicit strong convergence rates shown for numerical time approximations of the stochastic KdV.

</details>


### [115] [Optimal complexity of adaptive FEM for second-order linear elliptic PDEs driven by non-residual estimators, Part I: Symmetric PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14820)
*Philipp Bringmann,Aleksandar Dadic,Dario Ferloni,Gregor Gantner,Dirk Praetorius,Julian Streitberger*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了对称二阶线性椭圆型偏微分方程的自适应有限元方法，证明在抽象假设下，自适应算法（联合网格细化与迭代代数求解器）能够实现无条件全R线性收敛，且对于非残差估计量（如Zienkiewicz-Zhu型平均估计量或通量平衡估计量），在适当参数下可保证最优计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应有限元方法的收敛性与最优性分析主要集中于残差型后验误差估计量，而对工程中广泛使用的非残差估计量（如Zienkiewicz-Zhu平均估计量、平衡通量估计量）缺乏严格的理论分析。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 基于抽象的后验误差估计量和迭代求解器的假设条件，建立自适应算法（联合局部网格细化与代数求解器）的理论框架；通过证明无条件全R线性收敛性，并分析用户选择参数对最优复杂度的影响。

Result: 1) 证明了自适应算法在抽象假设下具有无条件全R线性收敛性，与用户选择的参数无关；2) 对于足够小的参数，可保证最优复杂度，即拟误差的衰减率在通常的非线性逼近类意义下关于总计算成本（时间）达到最优；3) 该结果适用于非残差估计量，拓展了已有理论的适用范围。

Conclusion: 本文建立的自适应有限元方法理论框架为工程中广泛使用的非残差后验误差估计量（如Zienkiewicz-Zhu型和平衡通量型）提供了严格的收敛性与最优复杂度保证，推进了自适应算法的理论完备性。

Abstract: We consider adaptive finite element methods for symmetric second-order linear elliptic PDEs, where the adaptive algorithm steers the local mesh refinement as well as an iterative algebraic solver. Under abstract assumptions on the underlying a-posteriori error estimator and the solver, we prove that the usual adaptive algorithm leads to unconditional full R-linear convergence, independently of the user-chosen adaptivity parameters. For sufficiently small parameters, this guarantees optimal complexity in the sense that the decay rate of an appropriate quasi-error is optimal with respect to the overall computation cost (and hence time) measured in terms of the usual nonlinear approximation classes. Unlike available results in the literature, the main focus is on the analytical understanding of non-residual estimators like averaging-based estimators as proposed by Zienkiewicz and Zhu or estimators based on equilibrated fluxes.

</details>


### [116] [Subgrid-Scale Parameterization in Burgers' Equation Using Structure-Preserving Neural Networks and Entropy Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14855)
*Aijaz Nazir,Ilya Timofeyev*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于机器学习的方法，利用结构保持神经网络和熵变量，为粗粒度偏微分方程模拟开发亚网格参数化方案。该方法通过解耦的神经网络架构将亚网格修正显式分离为保守通量势网络和涡粘性网络，在Burgers方程粗模拟中准确再现了全尺度系统的能量谱、时空相关函数和动力学特征，且对训练参数范围外的条件具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在粗粒度模拟中，亚网格尺度（SGS）过程的准确参数化是一个关键挑战。传统SGS模型往往缺乏普适性和物理保真度，亟需发展能够保留物理结构并准确捕捉多尺度相互作用的机器学习方法。

Method: 采用结构保持神经网络和熵变量来学习亚网格通量；设计解耦的神经网络架构，将亚网格修正显式分离为两个独立组件：保守通量势网络（Flux Potential network）和涡粘性网络（Eddy Viscosity network）。

Result: 该降阶框架保持了高物理保真度，能够准确再现全尺度系统的能量谱、空间和时间相关函数以及动力学特征；方法具有鲁棒性，适用于训练参数范围之外的参数条件。

Conclusion: 所提出的结构保持机器学习方法能够有效学习亚网格参数化方案，在保持物理一致性的同时具有良好的泛化能力，为偏微分方程粗粒度模拟提供了一种有前景的SGS建模途径。

Abstract: We present a machine learning approach for developing subgrid-scale (SGS) parametrizations in coarse simulations of partial differential equations. We utilize structure-preserving neural networks and entropy variables to learn subgrid fluxes in coarse simulations of the Burgers' equation. In particular, we employ a decoupled neural network architecture explicitly separating the subgrid corrections into two distinct components: a conservative Flux Potential network and an Eddy Viscosity network. We demonstrate that this reduced-order framework maintains high physical fidelity, accurately reproducing the energy spectrum, spatial and temporal correlation functions, and dynamical characteristics of the full-scale system. Furthermore, we show that our approach is robust and applicable to parameters outside the training regime.

</details>


### [117] [Fully discrete least-squares splitting scheme for the Monge-Ampère equation: finite element analysis and convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15024)
*Anna Peruso*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对二维Dirichlet Monge-Ampère方程的最小二乘分裂算法，建立了全离散格式的收敛性理论，证明了在离散Miranda-Talenti估计和Hessian重构逼近性质下迭代格式的局部收敛性及其极限在H^2范数下的最优阶收敛性，并通过数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小二乘分裂算法在实际计算中已被成功应用多年，但一直缺乏全离散分裂格式的收敛性理论。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 提出并分析了一个有限元框架，结合离散Hessian重构与行列式约束的局部投影。利用离散Miranda-Talenti估计和Hessian重构的标准逼近性质，证明了迭代格式的局部收敛性及其极限在H^2型范数下的最优阶收敛性。验证了多种格式：C^1协调格式（Argyris元）、C^0-内罚格式和DG格式（次数≥3）；当|u|_{H^3}足够小时也覆盖二次C^0-内罚和DG格式。

Result: 建立了全离散最小二乘分裂算法的收敛性理论，证明了局部收敛性和最优阶收敛性，数值实验验证了理论收敛速度。

Conclusion: 本文首次为最小二乘分裂方法提出了相关离散格式并进行了收敛性分析，填补了该领域长期存在的理论空白。

Abstract: The least-squares splitting algorithm for the Monge-Ampère equation has been used successfully in computations for several years, but a convergence theory for fully discrete splitting schemes of this type has remained unavailable. In this work, we introduce and analyze a finite element framework for smooth solutions of the Dirichlet Monge-Ampère equation in two dimensions. The proposed schemes combine a discrete Hessian reconstruction with a local projection onto the determinant constraint. Under a discrete Miranda-Talenti estimate and standard approximation properties of the Hessian reconstruction, we prove local convergence of the iterative scheme and optimal-order convergence of its limit to the exact solution in an $H^2$-type norm. We verify the estimates for conforming $C^1$ schemes, including the Argyris element, and for $C^0$-interior penalty and DG schemes of degree at least three; quadratic $C^0$-interior penalty and DG schemes are also covered when $|u|_{H^3(Ω)}$ is sufficiently small. To the best of our knowledge, these discretizations have not previously been proposed or analyzed for least-squares splitting methods. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical convergence rates.

</details>


### [118] [An Adaptive and Physics-Preserving Multiscale Method for Two-Phase Flow Simulations in High-Contrast Heterogeneous Porous Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15063)
*Junhao Huang,Eric Chung,Wing Tat Leung*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于高对比度多孔介质中不可压缩、不混溶两相流的自适应保物理多尺度方法，该方法将保物理的隐式压力显式饱和度格式（P-IMPES）与混合约束能量最小化广义多尺度有限元法相结合，核心是饱和依赖系数的自适应更新策略。理论分析证明了局部守恒性、相公式无偏性及边界保持性，并给出了速度和饱和度的误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对高对比度多孔介质中两相流模拟时，传统多尺度方法在更新多尺度空间上计算代价高昂的问题，提出一种自适应更新策略，仅当移动性加权系数变化超过预设容差时才重新生成多尺度空间，以平衡精度与计算效率。

Method: 采用P-IMPES耦合CEM-GMsFEM，核心是自适应更新算法：通过监测依赖于饱和度的总迁移率变化，在超过预设容差时触发多尺度空间重构；并引入局部后处理步骤恢复细网格质量守恒。

Result: 数值实验在不同高对比度渗透率场中验证了方法的物理性质，表明更小的自适应容差能改善饱和度近似，同时避免不必要的多尺度空间更新，证实了自适应策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保持物理性质（局部守恒、无偏性、边界保持）的前提下，通过自适应机制有效控制计算成本，并建立了包含自适应容差、粗网格尺寸、谱近似和前缘误差等贡献的误差估计理论框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose an adaptive physics-preserving multiscale method for incompressible and immiscible two-phase flow in high-contrast porous media. The method couples a physics-preserving implicit-pressure explicit-saturation scheme (P-IMPES) with the mixed constraint energy minimizing generalized multiscale finite element method. The core algorithmic component is an adaptive update strategy for the saturation-dependent coefficient. Since the effective permeability \(κ_n=λ_t(S_w^n)K\) depends on the evolving saturation through the total mobility, we introduce an adaptive update algorithm that monitors the variation of the mobility-weighted coefficient and regenerates the multiscale spaces only when a prescribed tolerance is exceeded. A local postprocessing step is further used to recover fine-grid mass conservation. The analysis is a central part of the paper. We prove local conservation for both phases, the unbiased property of the phase formulation, and bounds preservation under a suitable CFL condition. For the advection-dominated case, we establish velocity and saturation error estimates, which clearly identify the contributions from the adaptive tolerance, the coarse mesh size, the spectral approximation, and the front-layer error. Numerical experiments on different high-contrast permeability fields confirm the physical properties of the method and show that smaller adaptive tolerances improve the saturation approximation while avoiding unnecessary updates of the multiscale spaces.

</details>


### [119] [Residual-Based Time Discretization on Nonlinear Approximation Manifolds: Analysis and Gaussian Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15086)
*Eddy de Leon,Caroline Lasser*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究基于残差最小化的Hilbert空间演化方程的时间离散参数逼近，提出了两种残差公式化方法（先离散后参数化与先参数化后离散），在ζ-方法框架下建立了统一的误差分析，并给出了在Lipschitz、单边Lipschitz和耗散性假设下的一阶和二阶收敛结果。方法应用于高斯近似流形时，残差范数和梯度具有显式闭式解，无需空间离散化即可高效实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为基于低维非线性流形参数化表示的高维演化方程时间推进方法建立严格的误差分析理论，统一理解“离散-参数化”和“参数化-离散”两类方法的关系与区别。

Method: 在ζ-方法框架下，分别研究两种残差公式化方法：1) 先对演化方程进行时间离散，然后在低维非线性流形上进行参数化逼近；2) 先对Dirac-Frenkel变分原理进行离散，直接控制参数动力学。两种方法均通过最小化适当定义的残差来完成时间步进。

Result: 建立了统一误差分析，界限将时间离散化效应与残差最小化效应分离；在Lipschitz、单边Lipschitz和耗散性假设下获得一阶和二阶收敛率；变分公式化需额外的参数化映射条件化稳定性条件；高斯近似流形上残差范数和梯度具有闭式解。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种无需空间离散化、高效实现且具有理论收敛保证的数值方法，数值实验验证了理论收敛率以及残差精度对守恒性质的影响。

Abstract: We study time-discrete parametric approximations of evolution equations in Hilbert spaces based on residual minimization. The solution is represented by a parametrized ansatz belonging to a low-dimensional nonlinear manifold, and time stepping is performed by minimizing suitably defined residuals at each step. Two natural residual formulations are considered: discretization followed by parametrization of the evolution equation, and discretization of the Dirac--Frenkel variational principle governing the parameter dynamics. A unified error analysis is developed for both approaches within the family of $ζ$-methods. The resulting bounds separate the effects of time discretization from those of residual minimization and yield first- and second-order convergence under Lipschitz, one-sided Lipschitz, and dissipativity assumptions. For the variational formulation, additional stability conditions involving the conditioning of the parametrization map arise naturally. The framework is applied to Gaussian approximation manifolds, for which residual norms and gradients admit explicit closed-form expressions when polynomial operators are involved. This enables efficient implementation without spatial discretization. Numerical experiments for time-dependent Schrödinger equations illustrate the theoretical convergence rates and the influence of residual accuracy on conservation properties.

</details>


### [120] [Multiscale Mixed-Dimensional Simulation via Domain Decomposition and Non-Intrusive Neural Model Order Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15171)
*Nunzio Dimola,Piermario Vitullo,Paolo Zunino*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种域分解降阶模型（DD-ROM）方法，通过将全局问题分解为局部可学习算子，利用级联训练的非侵入式神经代理近似局部细尺度操作，并嵌入迭代求解器中，实现大规模多尺度问题的高效求解。该方法在混合维椭圆问题中验证了稳定性、准确性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学工程中的多尺度计算模型因全局问题组装或直接求解计算代价过高而受限。域分解方法虽能缓解这一问题，但传统方法仍依赖高保真局部求解器。本文旨在通过降阶模型进一步降低在线阶段的计算成本，使全局问题能在避免细尺度算子组装和局部高保真求解的前提下高效求解。

Method: 基于域分解将全局解映射转化为一族局部可表示算子，通过参考构型标识实现局部表示性；采用级联训练策略训练非侵入式神经代理以近似局部细尺度操作，并将其嵌入非重叠Robin-Robin区域分解迭代求解器；从扰动不动点迭代角度分析全局误差有界性。

Result: 在混合维椭圆问题（三维体域耦合一维线域）上的数值实验表明，该方法对未见微尺度几何的近似准确、稳定性好，且随子域数量扩展具有良好的可扩展性，在线阶段无需细尺度算子组装和局部高保真求解。

Conclusion: DD-ROM框架通过局部可学习代理有效替代了细尺度计算，实现了从全局难解到局部可学习的范式转换，在多尺度问题中具有广阔的应用前景。

Abstract: Many computational models arising in science and engineering exhibit a multiscale structure that makes the assembly or direct solution of the global problem computationally prohibitive. Domain Decomposition (DD) methods overcome this limitation by replacing the global problem with a sequence of coupled local problems, whose iterative solution reconstructs the global response. This work introduces a method in the family of Domain Decomposition Reduced Order Models (DD-ROMs), based on the observation that DD naturally localizes not only the solution operator but also its geometric and parametric dependence. The central idea is that DD transforms a globally intractable solution map into a family of locally representable operators learnable from affordable local data after identification with a common reference configuration, a concept that we formalize through the notion of local representability. Non-intrusive neural surrogates are then trained to approximate the fine-scale local operations and embedded into the iterative solver. The training algorithm is based on a cascaded strategy designed to match the distributions encountered by the deployed surrogate iteration. We interpret the resulting DD method as a perturbed fixed-point iteration and establish that the global error remains bounded by the surrogate approximation error. The framework is instantiated for mixed-dimensional elliptic problems coupling three-dimensional bulk domains with embedded one-dimensional inclusions, using a two-level non-overlapping Robin-Robin method. Numerical experiments show that the resulting DD-ROM is stable, achieves accurate approximation on unseen microscale geometries and features good scalability properties with respect to the number of subdomains, scaling to large size global problems while avoiding fine-scale operator assembly and local high-fidelity solvers in the online stage.

</details>


### [121] [Efficient higher-order local time integration for Friedrichs' systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15192)
*Marlis Hochbruck,Jonas Köhler,Malik Scheifinger*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对空间离散化后的线性Friedrichs系统，构建了一种高效的高阶局部时间积分方案，通过预处理Krylov子空间方法（如QMR）求解每个时间步中的线性系统，并设计了与细小网格单元直径无关的预处理器，理论分析和数值实验验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的局部隐式方法（如蛙跳格式与Crank-Nicolson方法的组合）无法适用于高阶时间积分，而实际问题中常遇到大部分网格单元较大、仅少数单元较小的情况，需要一种能高效处理此类多尺度网格的高阶时间积分方案。

Method: 采用预处理Krylov子空间方法（如准最小残差法QMR）求解每个时间步中的线性系统；基于Hochbruck和Sturm（2016）的局部隐式方法分析，利用Faber多项式和复逼近理论构造了一种预处理器，使得Krylov子空间方法达到指定精度所需的迭代次数与细小网格单元的直径无关。

Result: 预处理器每次应用的成本仅为求解一个小型线性系统，其维度对应网格精细部分（及其相邻粗网格）的自由度。当该维度远小于整体网格规模时，预处理器非常高效。数值实验验证了四阶Gauss-Legendre Runge-Kutta方法的理论结果。

Conclusion: 本文提出的高阶局部时间积分方案能够有效处理多尺度网格问题，预处理器设计保证了迭代次数的网格无关性，且该方法可推广至非线性问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct an efficient higher-order local time integration scheme for spatially discretized linear Friedrichs' systems. In particular, our interest is in problems where only a few of the mesh elements are small while the majority of the elements is much larger. The special combination of two methods like the leapfrog method on the coarse part of the mesh and the Crank-Nicolson method on the fine part as was done in Hochbruck, Sturm 2016 and Hochbruck, Köhler 2022 is not suitable for higher-order time integration. Therefore, we suggest to approximate the solution of the linear systems arising in each time step by a preconditioned Krylov subspace method, e.g., the quasi-minimal residual method by Freund and Nachtigal 1991. The techniques developed here for linear problems also carry over to nonlinear problems, where linear systems of the same type arise within a Newton-type iteration.
  Motivated by the analysis of locally implicit methods by Hochbruck and Sturm 2016, we show how to construct a preconditioner in such a way that the number of iterations required by the Krylov subspace method to achieve a certain accuracy is bounded independently of the diameter of the small mesh elements. We prove this behavior by using Faber polynomials and complex approximation theory.
  The cost to apply the preconditioner consists of the solution of a small linear system, whose dimension corresponds to the degrees of freedom within the fine part of the mesh (and its next coarse neighbors). If this dimension is small compared to the size of the full mesh, the preconditioner is very efficient.
  We conclude by verifying our theoretical results with numerical experiments for the fourth-order Gauss-Legendre Runge--Kutta method.

</details>
